            by technological iteration and enhanced product
            competitiveness, NEVs led the market recovery. Moreover,
            the elevated fuel prices driven by geopolitical tensions
            in the Middle East further widened the performance gap
            between NEVs and internal combustion engine vehicles
            (ICEs). According to CAAM data, China’s NEV production
            and sales in the first half of 2026 reached 7.438 million and
            7.446 million units, representing year-on-year growth of
            6.7% and 7.3%, respectively. The monthly penetration rate
            of NEVs in China has surpassed 60%. The accelerating
            transition from old to new growth drivers is profoundly
            reshaping the industry landscape.

the approach to the diversity of Board. The Company recognises          之方針。本公司認同董事會成員多元化對企業管治

approach to achieve Board diversity, so as to ensure that the           針，以確保董事會根據本公司業務所需具備適當的
Board members possess appropriate skills, experience and                技能、經驗及多元化觀點。本公司董事會及提名
diverse views necessary for the business of the Company. In             委員會在設定董事會成員組合時，會從多方面考慮
determining the Board composition, the Board and Nomination             董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文
Committee of the Company consider a range of diversity elements,        化及教育背景、專業經驗、技能及知識。董事會所
including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational      有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並以客觀準則考慮人
background, professional experience, skills and knowledge. All          選，充分顧及董事會多元化的裨益。

2.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF THE FINANCIAL                          2、   財務報表的編製基礎
     STATEMENTS
     The financial statements have been prepared in                      本財務報表按照財政部頒佈的《企業會計準
     accordance with the “Accounting Standards for Business              則 － 基本準則》以及其後頒佈及修訂的具體
     Enterprises – Basic Standard” issued by the Ministry of             會計準則、應用指南、解釋以及其他相關規
     Finance and the specific accounting standards, application          定（統稱「企業會計準則」）編製。此外，本財
     guidelines, interpretations and other relevant regulations          務報表還按照《公開發行證券的公司信息披
     issued and revised thereafter (collectively known as the            露編報規則第15 號 － 財務報告的一般規定》
     “ASBE”). In addition, relevant financial information as             披露有關財務信息。
     required under the “Compilation Rules for Information
     Disclosures by Companies Offering Securities to the Public
     No. 15 – General Provisions on Financial Reports” has
     been disclosed in the financial statements.


     The financial statements are prepared on a going concern            本財務報表以持續經營為基礎列報。截至二
     basis. As at 30 June 2026, the Group had net current                零二六年六月三十日，本集團淨流動負債為
     liabilities of RMB64,781,825,000. Based on the cash flow            人民幣64,781,825千元，根據本公司管理層
     forecast prepared by the management of the Company,                 編製的現金流預測，並考慮本集團經營活動
     and taking into account the net cash flow generated                 產生的現金流量淨額、擁有的金融信貸額度
     from the operating activities of the Group, available               以及資本性投入的速度，本集團董事認為，
     financial credit facilities and the expected rate of capital        本集團將有足夠的營運資金支付其自二零二
     investments, the directors of the Group are of the opinion          六年六月三十日起未來十二個月到期的負債
     that the Group will have sufficient working capital to              和義務，因此，本財務報告以持續經營為基
     meet its liabilities and obligations falling due within the         礎編製是適當的。
     next twelve months from 30 June 2026; therefore, it is
     appropriate to prepare the financial report on a going
     concern basis.

     3.      SEGMENT REPORTING                                             3、    分部報告
     (1)     Operating segment                                             (1)   經營分部

             For management purposes, the Group is organised into                出於管理目的，本集團根據產品和服務劃分
             business units based on their products and services. The            業務單元。本集團目前有兩個報告分部，分
             Group currently has two reportable segments as follows:             別如下：


             a)      the electronics and other products segment                  a)   電子及其他產品分部包括製造和銷售
                     principally comprises the manufacturing and                      外殼等手機及電子產品部件、AI算力
                     sales of components for mobile phones and other                  基礎設施等，並提供整機組裝服務；
                     electronic products (including casings) and AI
                     computing infrastructure, and the provision of
                     whole-unit assembly services;


             b)      the automobiles and related products and other              b)   汽車、汽車相關產品及其他產品分部
                     products segment comprises the manufacture and                   包括製造和銷售汽車、汽車相關的模
                     sale of automobiles and auto-related molds and                   具及零部件、汽車租賃和汽車的售後
                     components and automobile leasing and after sales                服務、汽車動力電池、鋰離子電池、
                     services, automobile power batteries, lithium-ion                光伏產品以及鐵電池產品、軌道交通
                     batteries, photovoltaic products and iron battery                及其相關業務。
                     products, rail transport and its related business.


             Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating            管理層出於配置資源和評價業績的決策目
             segments separately for the purpose of making decisions             的，對各業務單元的經營成果分開進行管
             about resources allocation and performance assessment.              理。分部業績以報告的分部利潤為基礎進行
             Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable                評價。該指標系對利潤總額進行調整後的指
             segment profit. The adjusted profit before tax is measured          標，除不包括公允價值變動損益、財務費用
             consistently with the Group’s profit before tax except that         （除租賃負債利息支出和匯兌損益）、營業外
             gains or losses arising from changes in fair value, finance         收入、其他收益、資產處置損失、營業外支
             costs (excluding interest expenses on lease liabilities             出、投資收益（除對聯營企業和合營企業的
             and exchange gains or losses), non-operating income,                投資收益外）、銷售房產收入、對應的成本
             other income, losses on disposal of assets, non-operating           和稅費以及本公司作為集團總部發生的管理
             expenses, investment income (excluding investment                   費用之外，該指標與本集團利潤總額是一致
             income from associates and joint ventures), income from             的。
             sales of properties, the corresponding costs and tax
             expenses, as well as administrative expenses incurred by
             the Company as the Group’s headquarter are excluded
             from such measurement.

             The numerator of basic earnings per share is determined            基本每股收益的分子以歸屬於本公司普通股
             based on the net profit for the Period attributable to             股東的當期淨利潤，減去歸屬於預計未來可
             ordinary shareholders of the Company, less net profit              解鎖限制性股票的淨利潤確定。
             attributable to the restricted shares expected to be vested
             in the future.

             The denominator of basic earnings per share is the                 基本每股收益分母為發行在外的普通股的加
             weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding.            權平均數。


             The numerator of diluted earnings per share is determined          稀釋每股收益的分子以歸屬於本公司普通股
             based on the net profit for the Period attributable to             股東的當期淨利潤確定，即分子在計算的時
             ordinary shareholders of the Company, which means that             候加回計算基本每股收益分子時已扣除的歸
             when calculating the numerator, net profit attributable to         屬於預計未來可解鎖限制性股票的淨利潤。
             the restricted shares expected to be vested in the future
             which are deducted in calculating the basic earnings per
             share are added back.

             The denominator of diluted earnings per share is equal to          稀釋每股收益的分母等於下列兩項之和：(1)
             the sum of: (1) the weighted average number of ordinary            基本每股收益中母公司已發行普通股的加權
             shares in issue of the parent company in basic earnings            平均數；及(2)假定稀釋性潛在普通股轉換為
             per share; and (2) increase in the weighted average                普通股而增加的普通股的加權平均數。
             number of ordinary shares assuming conversion of dilutive
             potential ordinary shares into ordinary shares.

             In calculating the weighted average number of ordinary             在計算稀釋性潛在普通股轉換為已發行普通
             shares increased upon conversion of dilutive potential             股而增加的普通股股數的加權平均數時，以
             ordinary shares into ordinary shares in issue, the dilutive        前期間發行的稀釋性潛在普通股，假設在當
             potential ordinary shares issued in previous periods are           期期初轉換；當期發行的稀釋性潛在普通
             assumed to be converted at the beginning of the Period;            股，假設在發行日轉換。
             the dilutive potential ordinary shares issued in the Period
             are assumed to be converted on the issue date.

                     On 17 March 2025, the Company convened the                        本公司於二零二五年三月十七日召開

                     Regarding Review of the BYD Company Limited                       二五年員工持股計劃（草案）>及其摘
                     2025 Employee Share Ownership Plan (Draft) and its                要的議案》及《關於審議<比亞迪股份
                     Summary and the Resolution Regarding the Review                   有限公司二零二五年員工持股計劃管
                     of Management Measures for BYD Company Limited                    理辦法>的議案》等相關議案。
                     2025 Employee Share Ownership Plan and other

              The Group has entered into derivative financial instruments           本集團與銀行訂立了衍生金融工具合同，為
              contracts which are the foreign currency forward                      外匯遠期合同，採用類似於遠期定價以及現
              contracts with banks and adopts valuation techniques                  值方法的估值技術進行計量。模型涵蓋了多
              similar to forward pricing and present value methods                  個市場可觀察到的輸入值，包括交易對手的
              for measurement. The model incorporates various                       信用質量、即期和遠期匯率和利率曲線。外
              market observable inputs including the credit quality of              匯遠期合同的賬面價值與公允價值相同。衍
              counterparties and foreign exchange spot and forward                  生金融資產的盯市價值，是抵銷了歸屬於衍
              rates and yield curve. The carrying amounts of foreign                生工具交易對手違約風險的信用估值調整之
              currency forward contracts are the same as their fair                 後的淨值。交易對手信用風險的變化，對於
              values. The mark to market value of derivative financial              套期關係中指定衍生工具的套期有效性的評
              assets is the net value after offsetting the credit valuation         價和其他以公允價值計量的金融工具，均無
              adjustment attributable to the default risk of derivative             重大影響。
              instrument counterparties. The change of counterparty
              credit risk has no significant impact on the evaluation of
              hedging effectiveness of designated derivatives in hedging

              fair value.


              For the wealth management products at fair value through              以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的理財產
              profit or loss and receivables financing, the Group will              品、應收款項融資，本集團會利用條款及風
              estimate their fair values by using a discounted cash                 險相類似的工具之市場利率按照貼現現金流

      (4)     The fair value measurement in Level 3                           (4)   第三層次公允價值計量

              For investments in restricted shares and unlisted equity              對於限售股票、非上市的權益工具投資，利
              instruments, the Group adopts the recent transaction                  用近期交易法或者採用估值技術來確定其公
              method or applies valuation techniques to determine                   允價值，估值技術包括市場乘數法、期權定
              their fair value, while valuation techniques include                  價模型等。其公允價值的計量採用了重要的
              market multiplier method, option pricing models, etc. The             不可觀察參數，比如企業價值 ╱ 收入（「EV/
              measurement of fair value uses significant unobservable               Revenue」）比率、平均企業價值 ╱ 息稅折舊
              parameters, including enterprise value-to-revenue (“EV/               前利潤比率、平均企業價值 ╱ 淨利潤比率、
              Revenue”) ratio, average EV/EBITDA ratio, average EV/                 流動性折扣、波動率、投資標的淨值等。持
              Net profit margin, liquidity discount, volatility, net value          續第三層次公允價值計量的金融工具對這些
              of underlying investment, etc. The financial instruments              不可觀察輸入值的合理變動無重大敏感性。
              measured at fair value in Level 3 on a recurring basis                本集團相信，以估值技術估計的公允價值及
              were not significantly sensitive to a reasonable change in            其變動，是合理的，並且亦是於資產負債表
              these unobservable inputs. The Group believes that the                日最合適的價值。
              estimated fair values based on the valuation techniques
